---
title: "@duxweb/lumi-admin API"
description: "@duxweb/lumi-admin 的全部公开方法、类型、参数和返回值。"
---

Native json-render Admin configuration and React application shell. 入口：`@duxweb/lumi-admin`、`@duxweb/lumi-admin/catalog`。共 295 个公开符号，由 TypeScript 入口自动生成。

## 函数

### activeAttributes

Attributes that take part in the matrix: named, with at least one value.

```typescript
activeAttributes(attributes: readonly SkuAttribute[]): SkuAttribute[]
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `attributes` | `readonly SkuAttribute[]` | 是 |  |

返回 `SkuAttribute[]`。

### AdminColorModeProvider

Keeps the reader's colour mode (light, dark or system), follows the system when asked to, and stores the choice.

```typescript
AdminColorModeProvider({ initialPreference, storageBase, children }: AdminColorModeProviderProps): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `__0` | `AdminColorModeProviderProps` | 是 |  |

`__0` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | 否 |  |
| `initialPreference` | `"light" \| "dark" \| "system"` | 是 |  |
| `storageBase` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The Shell's storage namespace (`lumi:<app id>:shell`). When given, a chosen mode is saved to `<storageBase>:preferences` at once, so a choice made before the Shell mounts (on the sign-in page) is the one it opens with. |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

### adminConfigItems

导入路径：`@duxweb/lumi-admin/catalog`

The items of a one-or-many entry, the single object named `default`.

```typescript
adminConfigItems<T extends { name?: string | undefined; }>(value: T | readonly T[] | undefined): Array<T & { name: string; }>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `value` | `T \| readonly T[] \| undefined` | 是 |  |

返回 `(T & { name: string; })[]`。

### AdminDocumentTitle

Sets the browser tab's title from the page and app names by the manifest's `documentTitle` template.

```typescript
AdminDocumentTitle({ page, app, template, children }: PropsWithChildren<AdminDocumentTitleProps>): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `__0` | `PropsWithChildren<AdminDocumentTitleProps>` | 是 |  |

`__0` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `app` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | 否 |  |
| `page` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The route's label; a page may replace it through useDocumentTitle. |
| `template` | `string \| false \| undefined` | 否 | A template with `{page}` and `{app}`; `false` leaves the title alone. |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

### AdminInclude

Another JSON page in place of this element. It loads when it first renders
and is cached by `src`; its state is its own, with `params` at
`/include/params`, and it starts afresh when `params` change. A page that
includes itself, directly or further down, and nesting past
`MAX_INCLUDE_DEPTH` render an error instead.

```typescript
AdminInclude({ src, params }: { src: string; params?: Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined; }): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `__0` | `{ src: string; params?: Record<string, unknown> \| null \| undefined; }` | 是 |  |

`__0` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `params` | `Record<string, unknown> \| null \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `src` | `string` | 是 |  |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

### AdminManifestAssistant

The assistant panel the manifest's `app.assistant` describes. Labels go
through the translator, so they can be message keys. A command without a
`prompt` reaches the `onEvent` listeners as `assistant.command`.

```typescript
AdminManifestAssistant({ config, additions }: { config: AdminAssistant; additions?: AdminAssistantAdditions | undefined; }): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `__0` | `{ config: AdminAssistant; additions?: AdminAssistantAdditions \| undefined; }` | 是 |  |

`__0` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `additions` | `AdminAssistantAdditions \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `config` | `{ endpoint: string; title?: string \| undefined; placeholder?: string \| undefined; resource?: string \| undefined; suggestions?: { label: string; id?: string \| undefined; prompt?: string \| undefined; icon?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined; mentions?: { ...; } \| undefined; commands?: { ...; }[] \| undefined; default...` | 是 | 创建 Lumi 上下文时使用的 Provider 与运行环境配置。 |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

返回 `ReactNode`。

### buildModules

Menu items gathered into the modules of a dual layout, each with its grouped items.

### buildSkuVariants

Regenerates the variant rows for `attributes`, carrying the entered price,
stock and fields over from `previous` where a combination survives. A row
whose attribute set changed inherits from the closest old row that agrees on
every shared attribute; the `sku` code is only inherited once, so codes stay unique.
`defaults` fills the fields a row did not inherit.

```typescript
buildSkuVariants(attributes: readonly SkuAttribute[], previous?: readonly SkuVariant[], defaults?: Record<string, unknown>): SkuVariant[]
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `attributes` | `readonly SkuAttribute[]` | 是 |  |
| `previous` | `readonly SkuVariant[]` | 否 |  |
| `defaults` | `Record<string, unknown>` | 否 |  |

返回 `SkuVariant[]`。

### composeAdminExtensions

Merges extensions in order: components join the Admin catalog and registry,
maps (handlers, data, auth and access providers, drawers, badges, upload and agent options)
merge key by key, lists (routes, assistant commands and @ tabs) concatenate — a command or tab with
the same value replacing the earlier one — every `onEvent` and
`onWorkspaceChange` listener is kept, and any other option is taken from
the last extension that sets it. A component, action or data provider name
registered twice is reported, and the later one wins; a handler named like
a built-in Admin action (`navigate`, `openDrawer`, `closeDrawer`) is refused
when the Admin is created.

```typescript
composeAdminExtensions(list: readonly AdminExtension[]): MountAdminExtensions
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `list` | `readonly AdminExtension[]` | 是 |  |

返回 `MountAdminExtensions`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProviders` | `Record<string, AccessProvider> \| undefined` | 否 | Access providers by `access` name, replacing the permission-string rule. |
| `agent` | `AgentConfig \| undefined` | 否 | Agent chat requests: the default endpoint, the data source and extra headers. |
| `assistant` | `ReactNode` | 否 | A panel beside the page, such as an AI assistant. The shell renders its frame and a header toggle; the content is entirely the host's. |
| `assistantCommands` | `{ value: string; label: string; description?: string \| undefined; icon?: string \| undefined; prompt?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | / commands added to the manifest's `app.assistant`. |
| `assistantDefaultOpen` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Whether the assistant panel starts open for a reader who has not toggled it yet; defaults to open. The reader's own choice is remembered. |
| `assistantMentions` | `AdminPromptMentions \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Records each accepted write and lists the record history; optional. |
| `authProviders` | `Record<string, AuthProvider> \| undefined` | 否 | Auth providers by `auth` name, replacing the simple convention for the panels that use it. |
| `catalog` | `Catalog<SchemaDefinition<SchemaType<string, unknown>, SchemaType<string, unknown>>, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `dataProviders` | `Record<string, DataProvider \| AdminRequestHooks> \| undefined` | 否 | Data providers by `data` name, reached with `dataProviderName`; `default` stands for the mounted panel's own backend. Build them on the `http` the extension context hands over, so they carry the reader's credentials, locale and tenant. An entry may instead give only `{ onRequest, onResponse }`: the provider the config already builds for that name keeps its address and gains the hooks. On the panel's backend they reach every request to it, sign-in, uploads, the agent, the event stream and served pages included. |
| `drawers` | `AdminRoutedDrawerRegistry \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `env` | `Record<string, string \| number \| boolean \| null> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `handlers` | `Record<string, ActionHandler> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `headerEnd` | `ReactNode` | 否 | Rendered on the header's trailing edge — the design's notifications bell and anything else app-wide the shell cannot supply the feature for. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | 翻译、切换语言和格式化。 |
| `liveProvider` | `LiveProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Pushes backend changes; with it, queries refetch what an event touches. |
| `loadPageModule` | `AdminPageModuleLoader \| undefined` | 否 | Loads a `page.module` route's component; defaults to `import()` and the module's default export. Each URL loads once. |
| `manifestLoader` | `LumiJsonLoader<{ app: { id: string; title?: string \| undefined; titleKey?: string \| undefined; subtitle?: string \| undefined; subtitleKey?: string \| undefined; documentTitle?: string \| false \| undefined; ... 8 more ...; header?: { ...; } \| undefined; }; routes: AdminRoute[]; }> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `menuBadges` | `Readonly<Record<string, ShellMenuBadge>> \| undefined` | 否 | Live counts beside menu items, keyed by route name. |
| `notificationProvider` | `NotificationProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws `notify` notices; without one the action is a no-op. |
| `onEvent` | `AdminEventHandler \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onWorkspaceChange` | `((value: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Called when the user picks another workspace in the sidebar switcher (`app.workspaces`). |
| `packKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Key of packed manifests, pages and themes, when the backend masks them with one. A backend may answer `{ "packed": "…" }` instead of readable JSON (see `packDocument` in `@duxweb/lumi/catalog`); it is unpacked either way, and this is only needed when a key was used. |
| `registry` | `ComponentRegistry \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `routes` | `RouteObject[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `shell` | `ComponentType<{ children?: ReactNode \| undefined; }> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `sidebarFooter` | `ReactNode` | 否 | Rendered at the foot of the navigation sidebar, below the menu. |
| `specLoader` | `LumiJsonLoader<Spec> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The tenant (workspace) requests belong to; queries refetch when it changes. |
| `themeLoader` | `LumiJsonLoader<{ mode?: "light" \| "dark" \| "system" \| undefined; primary?: string \| undefined; brand?: string \| undefined; radius?: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined; fontSize?: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined; ... 5 more ...; info?: string \| undefined; }> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws `toast` feedback and automatic error reports; without one they are no-ops. |
| `upload` | `UploadOptions \| undefined` | 否 | 全局上传地址、请求头和允许的目标来源。 |
| `validator` | `Validator \| undefined` | 否 | 统一的数据校验器，可接 AJV、Zod、Valibot 或自定义实现。 |

### createAdmin

Creates an Admin from a manifest and a Lumi context: routes, menu, account pages, shell and page loading. Render its `App`.

```typescript
createAdmin(options: CreateAdminOptions): LumiAdminReact
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` | `CreateAdminOptions` | 是 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `assistant` | `ReactNode` | 否 | A panel beside the page, such as an AI assistant. The shell renders its frame and a header toggle; the content is entirely the host's. |
| `assistantCommands` | `{ value: string; label: string; description?: string \| undefined; icon?: string \| undefined; prompt?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | / commands added to the manifest's `app.assistant`. |
| `assistantDefaultOpen` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Whether the assistant panel starts open for a reader who has not toggled it yet; defaults to open. The reader's own choice is remembered. |
| `assistantMentions` | `AdminPromptMentions \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `cacheKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `catalog` | `Catalog<SchemaDefinition<SchemaType<string, unknown>, SchemaType<string, unknown>>, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `context` | `LumiContext` | 是 | `createLumi` 创建的共享上下文。 |
| `createRouter` | `((routes: RouteObject[]) => DataRouter) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `drawers` | `AdminRoutedDrawerRegistry \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `frameOrigins` | `string[] \| undefined` | 否 | Sites `Iframe` elements and `page.iframe` routes may show; this site always may. Without it any https site may. |
| `handlers` | `Record<string, ActionHandler> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `headerEnd` | `ReactNode` | 否 | Rendered on the header's trailing edge — the design's notifications bell and anything else app-wide the shell cannot supply the feature for. |
| `loadPageModule` | `AdminPageModuleLoader \| undefined` | 否 | Loads a `page.module` route's component; defaults to `import()` and the module's default export. Each URL loads once. |
| `login` | `{ title?: string \| undefined; subtitle?: string \| undefined; badge?: string \| undefined; heroTitle?: string \| undefined; heroHighlight?: string \| undefined; heroDescription?: string \| undefined; ... 8 more ...; copyright?: string \| undefined; } \| undefined` | 否 | Copy, demo account and links of the sign-in page; see `AdminLoginConfig`. |
| `manifest` | `string \| { app: { id: string; title?: string \| undefined; titleKey?: string \| undefined; subtitle?: string \| undefined; subtitleKey?: string \| undefined; documentTitle?: string \| false \| undefined; ... 8 more ...; header?: { ...; } \| undefined; }; routes: AdminRoute[]; }` | 是 | 本地 Manifest 对象或远程 URL。 |
| `manifestAuth` | `"public" \| "protected" \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `manifestLoader` | `LumiJsonLoader<{ app: { id: string; title?: string \| undefined; titleKey?: string \| undefined; subtitle?: string \| undefined; subtitleKey?: string \| undefined; documentTitle?: string \| false \| undefined; ... 8 more ...; header?: { ...; } \| undefined; }; routes: AdminRoute[]; }> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `menuBadges` | `Readonly<Record<string, ShellMenuBadge>> \| undefined` | 否 | Live counts beside menu items, keyed by route name. |
| `onEvent` | `AdminEventHandler \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onWorkspaceChange` | `((value: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Called when the user picks another workspace in the sidebar switcher (`app.workspaces`). |
| `packKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Key of packed manifests, pages and themes, when the backend masks them with one. A backend may answer `{ "packed": "…" }` instead of readable JSON (see `packDocument` in `@duxweb/lumi/catalog`); it is unpacked either way, and this is only needed when a key was used. |
| `pages` | `{ endpoint: string; method?: "GET" \| "POST" \| undefined; data?: Record<string, unknown> \| undefined; } \| undefined` | 否 | Loads page JSON through the backend instead of as files: each page `src` that is not a full URL is sent as `path` to `endpoint` through the data provider, so it is signed and unwrapped like any other request. A `specLoader` you pass loads pages its own way. |
| `registry` | `ComponentRegistry \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `routes` | `RouteObject[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `shell` | `ComponentType<{ children?: ReactNode \| undefined; }> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `shellStorageKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `sidebarFooter` | `ReactNode` | 否 | Rendered at the foot of the navigation sidebar, below the menu. |
| `specLoader` | `LumiJsonLoader<Spec> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `theme` | `string \| { mode?: "light" \| "dark" \| "system" \| undefined; primary?: string \| undefined; brand?: string \| undefined; radius?: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined; fontSize?: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined; ... 5 more ...; info?: string \| undefined; } \| undefined` | 否 | 主题 JSON 或远程主题地址。 |
| `themeLoader` | `LumiJsonLoader<{ mode?: "light" \| "dark" \| "system" \| undefined; primary?: string \| undefined; brand?: string \| undefined; radius?: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined; fontSize?: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined; ... 5 more ...; info?: string \| undefined; }> \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `LumiAdminReact`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `App` | `ComponentType` | 是 |  |
| `catalog` | `Catalog<SchemaDefinition<SchemaType<string, unknown>, SchemaType<string, unknown>>, unknown>` | 是 |  |
| `clear` | `() => void` | 是 |  |
| `context` | `LumiContext` | 是 | `createLumi` 创建的共享上下文。 |
| `dispose` | `() => void` | 是 |  |
| `getMenu` | `() => LumiAdminMenuItem[]` | 是 |  |
| `getRouter` | `() => DataRouter \| undefined` | 是 |  |
| `ready` | `() => Promise<void>` | 是 |  |
| `refresh` | `() => Promise<void>` | 是 | 请求返回 401 后续期凭证；返回 true 时原请求会重发一次。 |
| `registry` | `ComponentRegistry` | 是 |  |

### createAdminHandlers

The handlers of the Admin's own actions: `navigate`, `openDrawer` and `closeDrawer`.

```typescript
createAdminHandlers(router: RouterProvider | undefined, drawer: AdminDrawerController): Record<string, ActionHandler>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `router` | `RouterProvider \| undefined` | 是 |  |
| `drawer` | `AdminDrawerController` | 是 |  |

`router` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `back` | `() => void` | 是 | Goes back one history entry. |
| `getCurrentPath` | `() => string` | 是 | The current path with its query. |
| `go` | `(config: GoConfig) => void` | 是 | Opens an address. |
| `parse` | `() => ParsedLocation` | 是 | The current address, taken apart. |
| `subscribe` | `((listener: () => void) => () => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Notifies when the address changes. |

`drawer` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `activeKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The target of this layer (inside a drawer) or of the top layer (elsewhere). |
| `close` | `() => void` | 是 | Closes this layer (inside a drawer) or the top layer (elsewhere), and any above it. |
| `layers` | `AdminDrawerLayer[]` | 是 | Every open layer, bottom first. |
| `open` | `(target: string, options?: string \| number \| AdminDrawerOpenOptions) => void` | 是 | Opens a registered drawer by name or a JSON page by address (`/pages/x.json`). From the page it replaces whatever is open; from inside a drawer it opens on top of that drawer. |
| `recordId` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `Record<string, ActionHandler>`。

导入路径：`@duxweb/lumi-admin/catalog`

The app's i18n provider with the Admin's own texts beneath it.

```typescript
createAdminI18nProvider(provider?: I18nProvider): I18nProvider
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `provider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 |  |

`provider` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `changeLocale` | `(locale: string, params?: unknown) => Promise<void> \| void` | 是 | Switches the locale. |
| `formatCurrency` | `((value: number, options?: Intl.NumberFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | An amount in the configured currency and unit. |
| `formatDate` | `((value: I18nDateValue, options?: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A date in the locale's form. |
| `formatDateTime` | `((value: string, options?: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | An ISO 8601 UTC string shown in the configured time zone. |
| `formatNumber` | `((value: number, options?: Intl.NumberFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A number in the locale's form; components use `Intl` without it. |
| `formatRelativeTime` | `((value: number, unit: Intl.RelativeTimeFormatUnit, options?: Intl.RelativeTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A relative time such as "3 days ago". |
| `getDirection` | `(() => LumiDirection) \| undefined` | 否 | The current locale's text direction. |
| `getFallbackLocale` | `(() => string \| undefined) \| undefined` | 否 | The locale a missing translation falls back to, when there is one. |
| `getFormatConfig` | `(() => I18nFormatConfig) \| undefined` | 否 | The currency and time zone settings. |
| `getLocale` | `() => string` | 是 | The current locale, e.g. `zh-CN`. |
| `subscribe` | `((listener: () => void) => () => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Notifies when the locale changes; with it `$t` and component text follow the switch. |
| `translate` | `(key: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>, defaultMessage?: string) => string` | 是 | The text for `key` in the current locale, with `{name}` placeholders filled from `params`; `defaultMessage`, else the key, when there is none. |

返回 `I18nProvider`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `changeLocale` | `(locale: string, params?: unknown) => Promise<void> \| void` | 是 | Switches the locale. |
| `formatCurrency` | `((value: number, options?: Intl.NumberFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | An amount in the configured currency and unit. |
| `formatDate` | `((value: I18nDateValue, options?: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A date in the locale's form. |
| `formatDateTime` | `((value: string, options?: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | An ISO 8601 UTC string shown in the configured time zone. |
| `formatNumber` | `((value: number, options?: Intl.NumberFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A number in the locale's form; components use `Intl` without it. |
| `formatRelativeTime` | `((value: number, unit: Intl.RelativeTimeFormatUnit, options?: Intl.RelativeTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A relative time such as "3 days ago". |
| `getDirection` | `(() => LumiDirection) \| undefined` | 否 | The current locale's text direction. |
| `getFallbackLocale` | `(() => string \| undefined) \| undefined` | 否 | The locale a missing translation falls back to, when there is one. |
| `getFormatConfig` | `(() => I18nFormatConfig) \| undefined` | 否 | The currency and time zone settings. |
| `getLocale` | `() => string` | 是 | The current locale, e.g. `zh-CN`. |
| `subscribe` | `((listener: () => void) => () => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Notifies when the locale changes; with it `$t` and component text follow the switch. |
| `translate` | `(key: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>, defaultMessage?: string) => string` | 是 | The text for `key` in the current locale, with `{name}` placeholders filled from `params`; `defaultMessage`, else the key, when there is none. |

### createManifestLoader

导入路径：`@duxweb/lumi-admin/catalog`

A loader of Admin manifests, validated against the Catalog.

```typescript
createManifestLoader(catalog: Catalog, options?: CreateLumiLoaderOptions & DocumentValidationOptions): LumiJsonLoader<AdminManifest>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `catalog` | `Catalog<SchemaDefinition<SchemaType<string, unknown>, SchemaType<string, unknown>>, unknown>` | 是 |  |
| `options` | `(CreateLumiLoaderOptions & { maxElements?: number; maxDepth?: number; directives?: readonly DirectiveDefinition[]; }) \| undefined` | 否 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `baseURL` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `cacheTime` | `number \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `directives` | `readonly DirectiveDefinition<z.ZodType<unknown, unknown, z.core.$ZodTypeInternals<unknown, unknown>>>[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `fetcher` | `((input: RequestInfo \| URL, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `headers` | `LumiRequestHeaders \| (() => LumiRequestHeaders \| Promise<LumiRequestHeaders>) \| undefined` | 否 | 请求头。 |
| `maxDepth` | `number \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `maxElements` | `number \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `packKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Key of packed answers, when the server masks them with one. A server may answer `{ "packed": "…" }` (see `packDocument`) instead of the document, so its text does not show in the network panel; the loader unpacks it before validating and caching. Readable JSON loads as before. |
| `timeout` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | 超时毫秒数。 |
| `trustedOrigins` | `string[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `LumiJsonLoader<{ app: { id: string; title?: string | undefined; titleKey?: string | undefined; subtitle?: string | undefined; subtitleKey?: string | undefined; documentTitle?: string | false | undefined; ... 8 more ...; header?: { ...; } | undefined; }; routes: AdminRoute[]; }>`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `invalidate` | `(url?: string, cacheKey?: string) => void` | 是 |  |
| `load` | `(input: string \| { app: { id: string; title?: string \| undefined; titleKey?: string \| undefined; subtitle?: string \| undefined; subtitleKey?: string \| undefined; documentTitle?: string \| false \| undefined; ... 8 more ...; header?: { ...; } \| undefined; }; routes: AdminRoute[]; }, options?: LumiLoadOptions) => Promis...` | 是 |  |
| `peek` | `(url: string, cacheKey?: string) => { app: { id: string; title?: string \| undefined; titleKey?: string \| undefined; subtitle?: string \| undefined; subtitleKey?: string \| undefined; ... 9 more ...; header?: { ...; } \| undefined; }; routes: AdminRoute[]; } \| undefined` | 是 |  |


| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `url` | `string` | 是 | 请求地址。 |

返回 `Promise<ComponentType<AdminModulePageProps>>`。

### isDrawerPageSource

A JSON page address a drawer may open: a path on this site (`/pages/x.json`)
or one relative to the manifest (`pages/x.json`). Another origin, a
protocol-relative or a scripted URL is refused, because the address arrives
from the location bar where anyone can write a link.

```typescript
isDrawerPageSource(target: string): boolean
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `target` | `string` | 是 |  |

返回 `boolean`。

### LumiAdminForgotPassword

Asks `authProvider.forgotPassword` for a reset link; the answer never says whether the address has an account.

```typescript
LumiAdminForgotPassword(props: LumiAdminForgotPasswordProps): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `props` | `LumiAdminForgotPasswordProps` | 是 |  |

`props` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `badge` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | A short label above the headline, such as the product line. |
| `copyright` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Defaults to `© <year> <title>`. |
| `features` | `readonly LumiAdminLoginFeature[] \| undefined` | 否 | Points listed under the headline, each an icon, a title and a line; pass `[]` to show none. |
| `help` | `{ label?: string; linkLabel: string; href: string; } \| undefined` | 否 | Where a reader who cannot get in finds help, at the top right. |
| `heroDescription` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `heroHighlight` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | A phrase of the headline drawn in the brand colour. |
| `heroTitle` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The headline beside the form (folded away on narrow screens). |
| `links` | `readonly { label: string; href: string; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | Links at the foot, such as privacy or terms. |
| `locales` | `readonly { value: string; label: string; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | Languages offered before sign-in; the switch shows from two up. |
| `loginHref` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The sign-in page, linked from every account page; defaults to `/login`. |
| `logo` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Image URL of the mark; without one the mark is the title's initial. |
| `navigate` | `((to: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Moves between the account pages without a reload; links load the page when left out. |
| `storageBase` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The Shell's storage namespace (`lumi:<app id>:shell`). The language chosen here is saved under it, so the Shell opens in the same language. |
| `subtitle` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `title` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Product name beside the mark; defaults to the `lumi.admin.title` message. |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

### LumiAdminLogin

The sign-in page: brand panel, form, demo-account hint and links, from the `login` config.

```typescript
LumiAdminLogin(props: LumiAdminLoginProps): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `props` | `LumiAdminLoginProps` | 是 |  |

`props` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `assignedNote` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The note under the card when there is no sign-up, e.g. that an administrator assigns accounts. |
| `badge` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | A short label above the headline, such as the product line. |
| `copyright` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Defaults to `© <year> <title>`. |
| `demo` | `{ username: string; password: string; } \| undefined` | 否 | A demo deployment's account, offered in a hint that fills the form. |
| `features` | `readonly LumiAdminLoginFeature[] \| undefined` | 否 | Points listed under the headline, each an icon, a title and a line; pass `[]` to show none. |
| `forgotPassword` | `false \| { href?: string; message?: string; } \| undefined` | 否 | `false` hides "Forgot password?"; `href` opens that page. Otherwise an auth provider with `forgotPassword` opens the built-in reset page, and one without shows `message` (or a default asking for the administrator) as a toast. |
| `help` | `{ label?: string; linkLabel: string; href: string; } \| undefined` | 否 | Where a reader who cannot get in finds help, at the top right. |
| `heroDescription` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `heroHighlight` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | A phrase of the headline drawn in the brand colour. |
| `heroTitle` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The headline beside the form (folded away on narrow screens). |
| `links` | `readonly { label: string; href: string; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | Links at the foot, such as privacy or terms. |
| `locales` | `readonly { value: string; label: string; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | Languages offered before sign-in; the switch shows from two up. |
| `logo` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Image URL of the mark; without one the mark is the title's initial. |
| `navigate` | `((to: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Moves between the account pages without a reload; links load the page when left out. |
| `onSuccess` | `((result: AuthActionResponse) => Promise<void> \| void) \| undefined` | 否 | 请求成功后的回调。 |
| `register` | `{ href: string; } \| undefined` | 否 | Where "Create account" leads; without it the card says accounts are assigned when `assignedNote` is set. |
| `remember` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Offer "Remember me" (default true); the choice reaches the auth provider's `login` as `remember`. |
| `storageBase` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The Shell's storage namespace (`lumi:<app id>:shell`). The language chosen here is saved under it, so the Shell opens in the same language. |
| `subtitle` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Line under the "Welcome back" heading. |
| `title` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Product name beside the mark; defaults to the `lumi.admin.title` message. |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

### LumiAgentPanel

The Shell's assistant panel connected to an agent endpoint: the open
conversation, earlier ones under the history button, and the composer. The
page being viewed goes with each message as context while it is shared.
Put it in the `assistant` slot of mountAdmin or createAdmin.

| `endpoint` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The chat endpoint; defaults to the configured `agent.endpoint`, then `/agent/chat`. |
| `mentions` | `AdminPromptMentions \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onCommand` | `((command: AgentCommand) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | A command without a `prompt` was picked; without this handler its label is sent as a message. |
| `placeholder` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `resource` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The DataProvider resource of the conversations: its list feeds `sessions`, `getOne` loads one. |
| `shareContext` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Whether the page being viewed is shared with the agent before the reader changes it; defaults to true. |
| `suggestions` | `readonly { key: string; label: string; prompt?: string; icon?: string; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | Prompts offered while the conversation is empty; `prompt` is the text sent, the label by default. |
| `title` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

### LumiArticle

The React component of the JSON `Article` element. A readable article: tags, title, standfirst, author with date and reading time, an optional cover figure, then the body in the Prose styles from Markdown or sanitized HTML. On wide screens a contents list of the body's h2 and h3 headings follows the reading position beside it. `actions` sits beside the byline (edit, share); `default` follows the body, for related records or comments.

| `children` | `ReactNode` | 否 | After the body: related records, comments. |

### LumiBarChart

A column chart drawn from the layout itself: no canvas, no library, and the
numbers stay in the DOM so a screen reader gets the series, not a picture.
One column carries the full tone and the rest a tint of it, so the reading
that matters stands out against dashed gridlines on a rounded scale.

```typescript
LumiBarChart({ points, ariaLabel, tone, highlight, caption, showValues, axis, max, formatTick, className }: LumiBarChartProps): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `__0` | `LumiBarChartProps` | 是 |  |

`__0` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ariaLabel` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `axis` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Shows the value scale beside the gridlines. |
| `caption` | `ReactNode` | 否 | One line under the plot, e.g. where the peak sits. |
| `className` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `formatTick` | `((value: number) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | Formats the axis ticks; defaults to a compact number in the active locale, e.g. `8K`. |
| `highlight` | `"none" \| "peak" \| (string & {}) \| undefined` | 否 | The column drawn in the full tone: `peak` is the largest value, a point `key` picks one column (e.g. today), `none` paints every column in full. |
| `max` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Top of the scale; rounded up from the series peak when omitted. |
| `points` | `readonly LumiBarChartPoint[]` | 是 |  |
| `showValues` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Prints each reading (its `display`, else the value) above its column. |
| `tone` | `"success" \| "warning" \| "danger" \| "info" \| "neutral" \| "primary" \| undefined` | 否 | Colour of the emphasised column; the other columns are a soft tint of it. |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

### LumiCodeExample

A documented example: a short explanation, then the source and the result it
produces. Code is always visible; the two panes share one frame and one
height, so a short result never leaves the code hanging past it.

| `actions` | `ReactNode` | 否 | Controls for the whole example, beside its title. |
| `align` | `"center" \| "start" \| undefined` | 否 | `center` places a small result in the middle of its pane instead of at the top. |

| `id` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 记录主键。 |

### LumiFunnelChart

A centred funnel drawn in SVG: each stage is a band whose width tapers down
the funnel, and trapezoid connectors join consecutive bands into one
silhouette. The bands step from the full palette colour to a lighter tint;
the connectors carry the stage-to-stage conversion. Names and counts sit in
aligned side columns (above each band in a narrow card), and an ordered list
gives screen readers every stage with its share, conversion and drop-off.

```typescript
LumiFunnelChart({ stages, ariaLabel, color, scale, className }: LumiFunnelChartProps): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `__0` | `LumiFunnelChartProps` | 是 |  |

`__0` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ariaLabel` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `className` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `color` | `2 \| 1 \| 4 \| 3 \| 5 \| undefined` | 否 | Chart palette colour (`--chart-1` … `--chart-5`) the bands step down from. |
| `scale` | `"linear" \| "sqrt" \| undefined` | 否 | How a stage's share of the first sets its band width. `sqrt` keeps small stages readable after a steep first drop; `linear` is strictly proportional. Either way a band never drops below a minimum width or widens down the funnel. |
| `stages` | `readonly LumiFunnelStage[]` | 是 | Ordered stages, widest first; each band is measured against the first stage. |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

### LumiMatrixChart

A daily trend drawn as a dot matrix: each day is a column of dots filled up
to its share of the scale, so a single bad day reads at a glance without the
false precision of a smooth line. Values stay in the DOM for screen readers.

| `className` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

| `labelEvery` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Prints every n-th axis label, starting from the first and ending on the last. |
| `max` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Top of the scale; rounded up from the series peak when omitted. |
| `points` | `readonly LumiMatrixChartPoint[]` | 是 |  |

| `description` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Reads under the number: what it is compared against or where it came from. Keep it to a few words — it repeats across the whole row. |
| `icon` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Leads the label; a registered icon name such as `receipt`. |
| `label` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `scale` | `"sm" \| "md" \| undefined` | 否 | `md` is the dashboard scale, where the number is the page. `sm` is the strip above a table, where the table is the page. |
| `series` | `readonly number[] \| undefined` | 否 | Recent history, oldest first. Drawn as mini bars; two points minimum. |
| `tone` | `"success" \| "warning" \| "danger" \| "info" \| "neutral" \| "primary" \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `unit` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Trails the value in a quieter face, e.g. `k` or `orders`. |
| `value` | `string \| number` | 是 |  |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

### LumiPage

The React component of the JSON `Page` element. Admin page surface. `stack` spaces several sections (cards, grids, tables) with the standard section gap.

| `className` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

| `stretch` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Takes the height the shell gives the page instead of the height its content happens to need — for pages whose foot is part of the design, like a wizard's step controls, which must not float halfway up an empty well. |

### LumiPipeline

One bar for the whole, one breakdown row per stage. A proportion list would
rank the stages against each other; a pipeline is about how one population
divides, so the stages share a single bar and the rows underneath carry the
counts and shares, like a disk usage bar. In a wide container the rows fold
into a legend grid under the bar.

```typescript
LumiPipeline({ stages, ariaLabel, className }: LumiPipelineProps): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `__0` | `LumiPipelineProps` | 是 |  |

`__0` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ariaLabel` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `className` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `stages` | `readonly LumiPipelineStage[]` | 是 |  |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

### LumiPreviewCard

A gallery tile: a fixed-ratio preview on a muted surface above a name and a
one-line description. Every tile in a row has the same height whatever its
preview holds.

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `badge` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | A short label over the preview's top-left corner, such as a category. |
| `className` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `description` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | One line; a longer text is truncated and stays whole in the tooltip. |
| `href` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Opens the subject. The title becomes a link stretched over the card. |
| `preview` | `ReactNode` | 是 | A static miniature of the subject. It is inert and hidden from assistive technology; the title names the card. |
| `previewScale` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Shrinks a miniature that is larger than the frame, e.g. `0.75`. |
| `title` | `string` | 是 |  |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

### LumiProportionList

A ranking: label and reading on one line, a thin solid bar on a track under
them, measured against the largest item so the order reads at a glance
without a legend. The share column turns the list into parts of a whole.

```typescript
LumiProportionList({ items, ariaLabel, tone, sort, showShare, valueTone, className }: LumiProportionListProps): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ariaLabel` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `className` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `items` | `readonly LumiProportionItem[]` | 是 |  |
| `showShare` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Adds each item's percentage of the total after its reading. |
| `sort` | `"none" \| "desc" \| undefined` | 否 | `desc` ranks the items by value with any `other` item last; `none` keeps the given order. |
| `tone` | `"success" \| "warning" \| "danger" \| "info" \| "neutral" \| "primary" \| undefined` | 否 | Default tone for items if not specified on item |
| `valueTone` | `"default" \| "muted" \| undefined` | 否 | `muted` sets the reading back as a secondary count, e.g. `9 tasks` beside a person; the bar carries the comparison. |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

| `aria-busy` | `Booleanish \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `aria-checked` | `boolean \| "true" \| "false" \| "mixed" \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates the current "checked" state of checkboxes, radio buttons, and other widgets. |

| `aria-colindex` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Defines an element's column index or position with respect to the total number of columns within a table, grid, or treegrid. |

| `aria-describedby` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Identifies the element (or elements) that describes the object. |
| `aria-description` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Defines a string value that describes or annotates the current element. |
| `aria-details` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Identifies the element that provides a detailed, extended description for the object. |

| `aria-expanded` | `Booleanish \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates whether the element, or another grouping element it controls, is currently expanded or collapsed. |

| `aria-labelledby` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Identifies the element (or elements) that labels the current element. |

### LumiSettingsPage

The React component of the JSON `SettingsPage` element. Bound settings navigation. Default children can use visible to select section content.

```typescript
LumiSettingsPage(props: LumiSettingsPageProps): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `props` | `LumiSettingsPageProps` | 是 |  |

`props` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `actions` | `ReactNode` | 否 |  |
| `defaultValue` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `description` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `dirty` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `leaveDescription` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `leaveTitle` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `meta` | `ReactNode` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `navLabel` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onChange` | `((value: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `sections` | `readonly LumiSettingsSection[]` | 是 |  |
| `sectionsDescription` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `sectionsTitle` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `state` | `LumiPageState \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `status` | `ReactNode` | 否 | Save state or validation feedback shown beside the section actions. |
| `title` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `value` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

### LumiSettingsRow

A settings group owns one card with a heading, fields and optional footer.

```typescript
LumiSettingsRow({ label, description, required, columns, actions, status, children, className, ...rest }: LumiSettingsRowProps): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `__0` | `LumiSettingsRowProps` | 是 |  |

`__0` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `about` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `accessKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `actions` | `ReactNode` | 否 | Actions and status owned by this group, rendered in its card footer. |
| `aria-activedescendant` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Identifies the currently active element when DOM focus is on a composite widget, textbox, group, or application. |
| `aria-atomic` | `Booleanish \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates whether assistive technologies will present all, or only parts of, the changed region based on the change notifications defined by the aria-relevant attribute. |
| `aria-autocomplete` | `"none" \| "both" \| "inline" \| "list" \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates whether inputting text could trigger display of one or more predictions of the user's intended value for an input and specifies how predictions would be presented if they are made. |

### LumiSkuEditor

Specifications on top, the combination matrix below. Editing the specifications
regenerates the matrix and keeps what was typed for combinations that remain.

```typescript
LumiSkuEditor(props: LumiSkuEditorProps): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `props` | `LumiSkuEditorProps` | 是 |  |

`props` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ariaLabel` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `className` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `columns` | `SkuColumn[] \| undefined` | 否 | Matrix columns; defaults to price (currency), stock (number) and sku (text). |
| `disabled` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `errorDisplay` | `"tooltip" \| "inline" \| undefined` | 否 | `tooltip` (default) keeps the matrix rows one height; `inline` writes errors under the controls. |
| `errors` | `Record<string, string> \| undefined` | 否 | Field errors such as submitForm's `fieldErrors`. |
| `labels` | `SkuEditorLabels \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `maxAttributes` | `number \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `name` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Error path of the bound value, e.g. `sku`: rows read `sku.variants.2.price`. |
| `onChange` | `((value: SkuValue) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onErrorsChange` | `((errors: Record<string, string>) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `precision` | `number \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `pricePrefix` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `readonly` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `value` | `Partial<SkuValue> \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

### LumiToggleRow

A settings switch is a row, not a field and not a card: the sentence explains
it on the left and the control answers on the right. Giving each one a card
would make six minor preferences look like six major objects.

```typescript
LumiToggleRow({ title, description, icon, controlId, children, error, className }: LumiToggleRowProps): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `__0` | `LumiToggleRowProps` | 是 |  |

`__0` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | 是 | The control itself: a switch, a compact select or a short input. |
| `className` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `controlId` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Id of the control on the right. The title becomes its `<label>` and the description its `aria-describedby` target (`${controlId}-description`). |
| `description` | `ReactNode` | 否 | One line on what turning it on actually does. |
| `error` | `ReactNode` | 否 | Field error under the description, linked as `${controlId}-error`. |
| `icon` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Manifest icon name shown before the title, e.g. `mail` or `bell`. |
| `title` | `ReactNode` | 是 |  |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。

### LumiTreeFilter

A side panel that narrows a list to one branch of a tree.

```typescript
LumiTreeFilter({ items, value, onChange, onSubtreeChange, title, allLabel, showAll, searchable, defaultExpandAll, loading, onLoadChildren, onSearch, ariaLabel, className }: LumiTreeFilterProps): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `__0` | `LumiTreeFilterProps` | 是 |  |

`__0` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `allLabel` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Label of the row that clears the filter. |
| `ariaLabel` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `className` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `defaultExpandAll` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `items` | `readonly CascaderOption[]` | 是 |  |
| `loading` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onChange` | `((value: OptionValue \| null) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onLoadChildren` | `((node: CascaderOption) => Promise<unknown> \| void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |

| `searchable` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `showAll` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `title` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

### mergeAssistantAdditions

The earlier commands and @ tabs followed by the later ones; a later tab,
command or option with the same value (an option: of the same kind)
replaces the earlier one, and sources
merge by type.

```typescript
mergeAssistantAdditions(earlier: AdminAssistantAdditions, later?: AdminAssistantAdditions): { commands: AgentCommand[]; mentions: AdminPromptMentions | undefined; }
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `earlier` | `AdminAssistantAdditions` | 是 |  |
| `later` | `AdminAssistantAdditions` | 否 |  |

`earlier` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `commands` | `readonly { value: string; label: string; description?: string \| undefined; icon?: string \| undefined; prompt?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `mentions` | `AdminPromptMentions \| undefined` | 否 |  |

`later` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `commands` | `readonly { value: string; label: string; description?: string \| undefined; icon?: string \| undefined; prompt?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `mentions` | `AdminPromptMentions \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `{ commands: AgentCommand[]; mentions: AdminPromptMentions | undefined; }`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `commands` | `{ value: string; label: string; description?: string \| undefined; icon?: string \| undefined; prompt?: string \| undefined; }[]` | 是 |  |
| `mentions` | `AdminPromptMentions \| undefined` | 是 |  |

### mountAdmin

Boots a complete Admin from JSON: the panel the address (or `options.panel`)
picks, with its backend, sign-in and permission rule, translations and formats
from `i18n`, menus and pages from its manifest. The same call backs the
prebuilt base script and a compiled host that adds its own extensions.

```typescript
mountAdmin(source: string | AdminBaseConfig, extensions?: MountAdminExtensions | ((context: AdminExtensionContext) => MountAdminExtensions | Promise<MountAdminExtensions>), options?: MountAdminOptions): Promise<MountedAdmin>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `source` | `string \| { data: { url: string; name?: string \| undefined; live?: string \| undefined; auth?: boolean \| undefined; } \| { name: string; url: string; live?: string \| undefined; auth?: boolean \| undefined; }[]; ... 11 more ...; cacheKey?: string \| undefined; }` | 是 |  |
| `extensions` | `MountAdminExtensions \| ((context: AdminExtensionContext) => MountAdminExtensions \| Promise<MountAdminExtensions>)` | 否 |  |
| `options` | `MountAdminOptions` | 否 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `panel` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The panel's name; otherwise the one whose path holds the address, else the default. |

返回 `Promise<MountedAdmin>`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `App` | `ComponentType` | 是 |  |
| `catalog` | `Catalog<SchemaDefinition<SchemaType<string, unknown>, SchemaType<string, unknown>>, unknown>` | 是 |  |
| `clear` | `() => void` | 是 |  |
| `config` | `{ data: { url: string; name?: string \| undefined; live?: string \| undefined; auth?: boolean \| undefined; } \| { name: string; url: string; live?: string \| undefined; auth?: boolean \| undefined; }[]; ... 11 more ...; cacheKey?: string \| undefined; }` | 是 | The validated config, with URLs resolved. |
| `context` | `LumiContext` | 是 | `createLumi` 创建的共享上下文。 |
| `dispose` | `() => void` | 是 |  |
| `getMenu` | `() => LumiAdminMenuItem[]` | 是 |  |
| `getRouter` | `() => DataRouter \| undefined` | 是 |  |
| `http` | `HttpClient` | 是 | The app's request client, signed by its auth provider. |
| `panel` | `AdminPanelSetup` | 是 | The mounted panel. |
| `ready` | `() => Promise<void>` | 是 |  |
| `refresh` | `() => Promise<void>` | 是 | 请求返回 401 后续期凭证；返回 true 时原请求会重发一次。 |
| `registry` | `ComponentRegistry` | 是 |  |
| `unmount` | `() => void` | 是 |  |

### resolveAdminBaseConfig

Reads the config: an object, or the URL of one, checked against the schema.
A config read from a URL resolves its own relative URLs against that file,
so a deployment below a subpath keeps working on every route. `overrides`
apply before validation, for what a compiled host supplies in code (for
example a manifest built from TypeScript).

```typescript
resolveAdminBaseConfig(source: string | unknown, overrides?: Partial<AdminBaseConfig> | ((loaded: Record<string, any>) => unknown)): Promise<AdminBaseConfig>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `source` | `unknown` | 是 |  |
| `overrides` | `Partial<{ data: { url: string; name?: string \| undefined; live?: string \| undefined; auth?: boolean \| undefined; } \| { name: string; url: string; live?: string \| undefined; auth?: boolean \| undefined; }[]; ... 11 more ...; cacheKey?: string \| undefined; }> \| ((loaded: Record<string, any>) => unknown)` | 否 |  |

返回 `Promise<{ data: { url: string; name?: string | undefined; live?: string | undefined; auth?: boolean | undefined; } | { name: string; url: string; live?: string | undefined; auth?: boolean | undefined; }[]; ... 11 more ...; cacheKey?: string | undefined; }>`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `access` | `{ name?: string \| undefined; separator?: ":" \| "." \| undefined; format?: string \| undefined; } \| { name: string; separator?: ":" \| "." \| undefined; format?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | The permission rules; `resource:action` when left out. |
| `auth` | `{ name?: string \| undefined; mode?: "bearer" \| "cookie" \| undefined; tokenKey?: string \| undefined; csrf?: { cookie: string; header: string; } \| undefined; data?: string \| undefined; endpoints?: { ...; } \| undefined; } \| { ...; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | The sign-ins; one with the default endpoints when left out. |
| `basename` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Where the site is served when not at the root, e.g. `/console`; every panel path is below it. |
| `cacheKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Storage namespace for the manifest cache and workspace; each panel keeps its own below it. |
| `data` | `{ url: string; name?: string \| undefined; live?: string \| undefined; auth?: boolean \| undefined; } \| { name: string; url: string; live?: string \| undefined; auth?: boolean \| undefined; }[]` | 是 | The backends. |
| `defaultPanel` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The panel `/` opens; defaults to the first. |
| `el` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | CSS selector of the mount node; defaults to `#app`. |
| `frameOrigins` | `string[] \| undefined` | 否 | Sites an `Iframe` element or a `page.iframe` route may show, e.g. `["https://report.example.com"]`; this site always may. Without it any https site may. |
| `i18n` | `{ locale: string; fallbackLocale?: string \| undefined; translations?: Record<string, string \| Record<string, string>> \| undefined; format?: { currency: string; currencyUnit: "major" \| "minor"; timeZone: string; dateTimeInput: "iso-utc"; } \| undefined; } \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `modules` | `string[] \| undefined` | 否 | Extension modules the base imports before it mounts: plain ES modules whose default export receives the runtime (React, Lumi, UI, Admin, zod) and returns the components, actions and callbacks they add. |
| `packKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Key of packed manifests, pages and themes. The backend may answer `{ "packed": "…" }` instead of readable JSON so the text does not show in the network panel; that works without any setting here, and this is the key when the backend masks the packed bytes with one. It hides documents from a casual look only: this file is itself readable. |
| `panels` | `{ manifest: string \| { app: { id: string; title?: string \| undefined; titleKey?: string \| undefined; subtitle?: string \| undefined; subtitleKey?: string \| undefined; documentTitle?: string \| false \| undefined; ... 8 more ...; header?: { ...; } \| undefined; }; routes: AdminRoute[]; }; ... 11 more ...; shellStorageKey...` | 是 | The back offices this deployment serves, chosen by path. |
| `theme` | `string \| { mode?: "light" \| "dark" \| "system" \| undefined; primary?: string \| undefined; brand?: string \| undefined; radius?: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined; fontSize?: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined; ... 5 more ...; info?: string \| undefined; } \| undefined` | 否 | Theme every panel uses unless it names its own. |

### resolveAdminSource

Resolves a page, module or logo address against the manifest's URL; rooted paths and full URLs stay as written.


| --- | --- | --- | --- |

返回 `string`。

### selectAdminPanel

导入路径：`@duxweb/lumi-admin/catalog`

The panel a page shows: the one named `panel` when given (a page of its
own), else the one whose path holds `pathname` (the longest wins), else the
default panel.

```typescript
selectAdminPanel(config: AdminBaseConfig, pathname: string, panel?: string): AdminPanelSetup
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `config` | `{ data: { url: string; name?: string \| undefined; live?: string \| undefined; auth?: boolean \| undefined; } \| { name: string; url: string; live?: string \| undefined; auth?: boolean \| undefined; }[]; ... 11 more ...; cacheKey?: string \| undefined; }` | 是 | 创建 Lumi 上下文时使用的 Provider 与运行环境配置。 |
| `pathname` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `panel` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

`config` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `access` | `{ name?: string \| undefined; separator?: ":" \| "." \| undefined; format?: string \| undefined; } \| { name: string; separator?: ":" \| "." \| undefined; format?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | The permission rules; `resource:action` when left out. |
| `auth` | `{ name?: string \| undefined; mode?: "bearer" \| "cookie" \| undefined; tokenKey?: string \| undefined; csrf?: { cookie: string; header: string; } \| undefined; data?: string \| undefined; endpoints?: { ...; } \| undefined; } \| { ...; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | The sign-ins; one with the default endpoints when left out. |
| `basename` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Where the site is served when not at the root, e.g. `/console`; every panel path is below it. |
| `cacheKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Storage namespace for the manifest cache and workspace; each panel keeps its own below it. |
| `data` | `{ url: string; name?: string \| undefined; live?: string \| undefined; auth?: boolean \| undefined; } \| { name: string; url: string; live?: string \| undefined; auth?: boolean \| undefined; }[]` | 是 | The backends. |
| `defaultPanel` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The panel `/` opens; defaults to the first. |
| `el` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | CSS selector of the mount node; defaults to `#app`. |
| `frameOrigins` | `string[] \| undefined` | 否 | Sites an `Iframe` element or a `page.iframe` route may show, e.g. `["https://report.example.com"]`; this site always may. Without it any https site may. |
| `i18n` | `{ locale: string; fallbackLocale?: string \| undefined; translations?: Record<string, string \| Record<string, string>> \| undefined; format?: { currency: string; currencyUnit: "major" \| "minor"; timeZone: string; dateTimeInput: "iso-utc"; } \| undefined; } \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `modules` | `string[] \| undefined` | 否 | Extension modules the base imports before it mounts: plain ES modules whose default export receives the runtime (React, Lumi, UI, Admin, zod) and returns the components, actions and callbacks they add. |
| `packKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Key of packed manifests, pages and themes. The backend may answer `{ "packed": "…" }` instead of readable JSON so the text does not show in the network panel; that works without any setting here, and this is the key when the backend masks the packed bytes with one. It hides documents from a casual look only: this file is itself readable. |
| `panels` | `{ manifest: string \| { app: { id: string; title?: string \| undefined; titleKey?: string \| undefined; subtitle?: string \| undefined; subtitleKey?: string \| undefined; documentTitle?: string \| false \| undefined; ... 8 more ...; header?: { ...; } \| undefined; }; routes: AdminRoute[]; }; ... 11 more ...; shellStorageKey...` | 是 | The back offices this deployment serves, chosen by path. |
| `theme` | `string \| { mode?: "light" \| "dark" \| "system" \| undefined; primary?: string \| undefined; brand?: string \| undefined; radius?: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined; fontSize?: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined; ... 5 more ...; info?: string \| undefined; } \| undefined` | 否 | Theme every panel uses unless it names its own. |

返回 `AdminPanelSetup`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `access` | `AdminAccessConfig` | 是 |  |
| `auth` | `AdminAuthConfig` | 是 |  |
| `basename` | `string` | 是 | Router base of the panel: `basename` joined with its path. |
| `data` | `AdminDataConfig[]` | 是 | Every backend; the panel's own is `default` to its pages. |
| `defaultData` | `AdminDataConfig` | 是 | The panel's backend. |
| `panel` | `AdminPanelConfig` | 是 |  |

### useAdminDrawer

The routed drawer controller: `open(target, options?)`, `close()`, the open layers and this layer's key and record.

```typescript
useAdminDrawer(): AdminDrawerController
```

返回 `AdminDrawerController`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `activeKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The target of this layer (inside a drawer) or of the top layer (elsewhere). |
| `close` | `() => void` | 是 | Closes this layer (inside a drawer) or the top layer (elsewhere), and any above it. |
| `layers` | `AdminDrawerLayer[]` | 是 | Every open layer, bottom first. |
| `open` | `(target: string, options?: string \| number \| AdminDrawerOpenOptions) => void` | 是 | Opens a registered drawer by name or a JSON page by address (`/pages/x.json`). From the page it replaces whatever is open; from inside a drawer it opens on top of that drawer. |
| `recordId` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

### useAdminDrawerChrome

Lets a routed drawer's content set its title and footer.

```typescript
useAdminDrawerChrome(chrome: AdminDrawerChrome): void
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `chrome` | `AdminDrawerChrome` | 是 |  |

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `description` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `footerEnd` | `ReactNode` | 否 |  |
| `footerStart` | `ReactNode` | 否 |  |
| `revision` | `string` | 是 | Identity of this chrome snapshot. Change it when the footer or title should refresh; the host stores the nodes from that render. |
| `title` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `void`。

| `layers` | `AdminDrawerLayer[]` | 是 | Every open layer, bottom first. |
| `open` | `(target: string, options?: string \| number \| AdminDrawerOpenOptions) => void` | 是 | Opens a registered drawer by name or a JSON page by address (`/pages/x.json`). From the page it replaces whatever is open; from inside a drawer it opens on top of that drawer. |
| `recordId` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

### useAgentConversation

One conversation with an agent and the list it belongs to, ready to spread
onto Lumi UI's ChatThread, PromptInput and AgentSessionList. The Admin
AgentWorkspace and AgentPanel are drawn from it; hosts with their own
layout use it the same way.

```typescript
useAgentConversation(options?: UseAgentConversationOptions): AgentConversation
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` | `UseAgentConversationOptions` | 否 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `body` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Extra fields sent with every request, e.g. the model. |
| `commands` | `{ value: string; label: string; description?: string \| undefined; icon?: string \| undefined; prompt?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | Slash commands in the composer. One with a `prompt` fills the box; others reach `onCommand`. |
| `context` | `{ id: string; label: string; kind?: "record" \| "file" \| "page" \| "selection" \| "data" \| undefined; icon?: string \| undefined; description?: string \| undefined; data?: Record<string, unknown> \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | Context sent with every message, e.g. the page being viewed. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `endpoint` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The chat endpoint; defaults to the configured `agent.endpoint`, then `/agent/chat`. |
| `mentions` | `AdminPromptMentions \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onCommand` | `((command: AgentCommand) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | A command without a `prompt` was picked; without this handler its label is sent as a message. |
| `onSessionChange` | `((id: string \| null) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Called with the conversation to open: one picked from the list, a new one (`null`), or a started one's id. |
| `resource` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The DataProvider resource of the conversations: its list feeds `sessions`, `getOne` loads one. |
| `searchDelay` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Milliseconds the conversation search waits for typing to pause. |
| `sessionId` | `string \| null \| undefined` | 否 | The open conversation, when the host keeps it (say in the address); leave it out to keep it here. |

返回 `AgentConversation`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `chat` | `AgentChat` | 是 |  |
| `draft` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `open` | `(id: string \| null) => void` | 是 |  |
| `prompt` | `{ value: string; streaming: boolean; onChange: (value: string) => void; onSubmit: (value: string) => void; onStop: () => void; onSuggestion: (suggestion: AgentSuggestion) => void; ... 7 more ...; onApprovalModeChange: (mode: "ask" \| "auto") => void; }` | 是 | The composer, as PromptInput takes it. |
| `reset` | `() => void` | 是 | Starts a new conversation. |
| `send` | `(text?: string) => void` | 是 | Sends `text`, or the draft, with the context; the draft clears once the message is accepted. |
| `sessionId` | `string \| null` | 是 | The open conversation's id, `null` for a new one not yet sent. |
| `sessions` | `{ sessions: AgentSession[]; value: string \| null; query: string; loading: boolean; remote: true; onQueryChange: (query: string) => void; onChange: (id: string) => void; onCreate: () => void; }` | 是 | The conversations, as AgentSessionList takes them. |
| `setDraft` | `(draft: string) => void` | 是 |  |
| `thread` | `Required<Pick<AgentMessageCallbacks, "onApprove" \| "onReject" \| "onAnswer" \| "onSkip" \| "onRegenerate" \| "onEditMessage" \| "onSuggestion">> & { ...; }` | 是 | The messages and every reader action, as ChatThread takes them. |
| `title` | `string \| undefined` | 是 | The open conversation's title, from the list. |

### useAssistantContext

Tells the assistant about `items` while the calling component is mounted:
they are offered as context the reader can remove before sending. Pass
nothing (or an empty list) when there is nothing to share.

```typescript
useAssistantContext(items: readonly AgentContextItem[] | undefined): void
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `items` | `readonly { id: string; label: string; kind?: "record" \| "file" \| "page" \| "selection" \| "data" \| undefined; icon?: string \| undefined; description?: string \| undefined; data?: Record<string, unknown> \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 是 |  |

返回 `void`。

### useAssistantContextItems

Everything registered for the assistant right now.

```typescript
useAssistantContextItems(): readonly AgentContextItem[]
```

返回 `readonly { id: string; label: string; kind?: "record" | "file" | "page" | "selection" | "data" | undefined; icon?: string | undefined; description?: string | undefined; data?: Record<string, unknown> | undefined; }[]`。

### useDocumentTitle

Names the current page in the browser tab, in place of its menu label, while
the calling component is mounted, e.g. with the record's name on a detail page.

```typescript
useDocumentTitle(title: string | undefined): void
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `title` | `string \| undefined` | 是 |  |

返回 `void`。

### useHideShellAssistant

Hides the shell's assistant panel and its toggle while the calling page is mounted, e.g. a page that is itself a conversation.

```typescript
useHideShellAssistant(): void
```

返回 `void`。

### useShell

The shell's state and actions; throws outside `LumiAdminShellProvider`.

```typescript

```

返回 `ShellContextValue`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `active` | `{ pathname: string; item?: LumiAdminMenuItem; group?: LumiAdminMenuGroup; module?: LumiAdminModule; breadcrumbs: readonly ShellBreadcrumb[]; }` | 是 |  |
| `app` | `Pick<{ id: string; title?: string \| undefined; titleKey?: string \| undefined; subtitle?: string \| undefined; subtitleKey?: string \| undefined; documentTitle?: string \| false \| undefined; ... 8 more ...; header?: { ...; } \| undefined; }, "id" \| ... 5 more ... \| "header"> & { ...; }` | 是 |  |
| `assistant` | `{ available: boolean; open: boolean; toggle: () => void; }` | 是 | The host-provided assistant panel beside the page; `available` is false when the host supplies none. |
| `auth` | `{ available: boolean; pending: boolean; error?: unknown; logout: () => Promise<void>; }` | 是 |  |
| `colorMode` | `{ preference: ShellColorModePreference; resolved: Exclude<ShellColorModePreference, "system">; set: (value: ShellColorModePreference) => void; toggle: () => void; }` | 是 |  |
| `groups` | `readonly LumiAdminMenuGroup[]` | 是 |  |
| `identity` | `{ value?: AdminIdentity; pending: boolean; error?: unknown; }` | 是 |  |
| `locale` | `{ value: string; options: readonly { value: string; label: string; }[]; set: (locale: string) => void; }` | 是 | The active language and those the app offers; `set` persists the choice. |
| `menu` | `readonly AdminMenuItem[]` | 是 |  |
| `modules` | `readonly LumiAdminModule[]` | 是 |  |
| `navigation` | `{ query: string; collapsed: boolean; mobileOpen: boolean; setQuery: (value: string) => void; toggleCollapsed: () => void; openMobile: () => void; closeMobile: () => void; navigate: (path: string) => void; }` | 是 |  |
| `tabs` | `{ enabled: boolean; items: readonly ShellTab[]; activeKey?: string; activate: (key: string) => void; close: (key: string) => void; closeOthers: (key: string) => void; closeAll: () => void; reload: (key: string) => void; }` | 是 |  |
| `workspace` | `{ options: readonly AdminWorkspace[]; current?: AdminWorkspace; select: (value: string) => void; }` | 是 | The workspaces the sidebar head switches between; a switcher from two up. |

### useShellContextPanel

Claims the context column while mounted and returns the element to portal
into, or null when there is none to show: the single layout, a collapsed
sidebar, or a screen too narrow for the column. A page then draws the
content itself.

```typescript
useShellContextPanel(): HTMLElement | null
```

返回 `HTMLElement | null`。

## 常量

### adminAccessConfigSchema

导入路径：`@duxweb/lumi-admin/catalog`

How menus, routes and `CanAccess` read the permissions login returns:
`{ "separator": "." }` turns `system/users` + `delete` into
`system.users.delete`; `*` covers a segment, a trailing `*` all below.

```typescript
const adminAccessConfigSchema: z.ZodUnion<readonly [z.ZodObject<{ name: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>; } & { separator: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodEnum<{ ":": ":"; ".": "."; }>>; format: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>; }, z.core.$strict>, z.ZodArray<...>]>
```

### adminPagesSourceSchema

导入路径：`@duxweb/lumi-admin/catalog`

Page JSON from the backend: each page `src` that is not a full URL is sent
as `path` (with `data` beside it) to `endpoint` through the data provider,
so it is signed and unwrapped like any other request. `method` defaults to
`POST`; `GET` sends the values as the query.

```typescript
const adminPagesSourceSchema: z.ZodObject<{ endpoint: z.ZodString; method: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodEnum<{ GET: "GET"; POST: "POST"; }>>; data: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodRecord<z.ZodString, z.ZodUnknown>>; }, z.core.$strict>
```

### adminTranslations

导入路径：`@duxweb/lumi-admin/catalog`

```typescript
const adminTranslations: I18nTranslations
```

### MAX_DRAWER_LAYERS

Deeper stacks replace the top layer instead of growing.

```typescript
const MAX_DRAWER_LAYERS: 3
```

### MAX_INCLUDE_DEPTH

How deep includes may nest; a deeper one renders an error instead of loading.

```typescript
const MAX_INCLUDE_DEPTH: 8
```

导入路径：`@duxweb/lumi-admin/catalog`

The composer's @ references; `sources` searches one DataProvider resource per tab, keyed by its `value`.


What an extension is built with beside the runtime.

```typescript

```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |

| `http` | `HttpClient` | 是 | The app's request client at the panel's backend, signed by the auth provider the Admin ends up with. `http.extend({ baseUrl })` reaches another backend; `auth: false` sends no app credentials there. |
| `panel` | `AdminPanelSetup` | 是 | The panel being mounted, with the auth and access it uses. `panel.data` lists every backend by name whichever way `data` was written, e.g. to read one's address. |

| `handlers` | `Record<string, ActionHandler> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `headerEnd` | `ReactNode` | 否 | Rendered on the header's trailing edge — the design's notifications bell and anything else app-wide the shell cannot supply the feature for. |
| `loadPageModule` | `AdminPageModuleLoader \| undefined` | 否 | Loads a `page.module` route's component; defaults to `import()` and the module's default export. Each URL loads once. |
| `login` | `{ title?: string \| undefined; subtitle?: string \| undefined; badge?: string \| undefined; heroTitle?: string \| undefined; heroHighlight?: string \| undefined; heroDescription?: string \| undefined; ... 8 more ...; copyright?: string \| undefined; } \| undefined` | 否 | Copy, demo account and links of the sign-in page; see `AdminLoginConfig`. |
| `manifest` | `string \| { app: { id: string; title?: string \| undefined; titleKey?: string \| undefined; subtitle?: string \| undefined; subtitleKey?: string \| undefined; documentTitle?: string \| false \| undefined; ... 8 more ...; header?: { ...; } \| undefined; }; routes: AdminRoute[]; }` | 是 | 本地 Manifest 对象或远程 URL。 |
| `manifestAuth` | `"public" \| "protected" \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `manifestLoader` | `LumiJsonLoader<{ app: { id: string; title?: string \| undefined; titleKey?: string \| undefined; subtitle?: string \| undefined; subtitleKey?: string \| undefined; documentTitle?: string \| false \| undefined; ... 8 more ...; header?: { ...; } \| undefined; }; routes: AdminRoute[]; }> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `menuBadges` | `Readonly<Record<string, ShellMenuBadge>> \| undefined` | 否 | Live counts beside menu items, keyed by route name. |
| `onEvent` | `AdminEventHandler \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onWorkspaceChange` | `((value: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Called when the user picks another workspace in the sidebar switcher (`app.workspaces`). |
| `packKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Key of packed manifests, pages and themes, when the backend masks them with one. A backend may answer `{ "packed": "…" }` instead of readable JSON (see `packDocument` in `@duxweb/lumi/catalog`); it is unpacked either way, and this is only needed when a key was used. |
| `pages` | `{ endpoint: string; method?: "GET" \| "POST" \| undefined; data?: Record<string, unknown> \| undefined; } \| undefined` | 否 | Loads page JSON through the backend instead of as files: each page `src` that is not a full URL is sent as `path` to `endpoint` through the data provider, so it is signed and unwrapped like any other request. A `specLoader` you pass loads pages its own way. |

| `shellStorageKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `sidebarFooter` | `ReactNode` | 否 | Rendered at the foot of the navigation sidebar, below the menu. |
| `specLoader` | `LumiJsonLoader<Spec> \| undefined` | 否 |  |

| `themeLoader` | `LumiJsonLoader<{ mode?: "light" \| "dark" \| "system" \| undefined; primary?: string \| undefined; brand?: string \| undefined; radius?: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined; fontSize?: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined; ... 5 more ...; info?: string \| undefined; }> \| undefined` | 否 |  |

| `title` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Product name beside the mark; defaults to the `lumi.admin.title` message. |

| `suggestions` | `readonly { key: string; label: string; prompt?: string; icon?: string; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | Prompts offered while the conversation is empty; `prompt` is the text sent, the label by default. |

### LumiAssistantMessage

One message of the conversation: the agent message contract (`parts` with
text, reasoning, tool calls, approvals, plans, diffs and sources) plus an
optional host-rendered `content` shown after the parts.

```typescript
interface LumiAssistantMessage
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `content` | `ReactNode` | 否 |  |
| `createdAt` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `errorText` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Shown with `status: "error"`. |
| `feedback` | `"up" \| "down" \| null \| undefined` | 否 | The reader's rating of an assistant answer. |
| `id` | `string \| number` | 是 | 记录主键。 |
| `parts` | `({ type: "text"; text: string; id?: string \| undefined; state?: "streaming" \| "done" \| undefined; } \| { type: "reasoning"; id?: string \| undefined; text?: string \| undefined; state?: "streaming" \| "done" \| undefined; label?: string \| undefined; steps?: { ...; }[] \| undefined; duration?: number \| undefined; } \| ... 7...` | 否 |  |
| `role` | `"system" \| "user" \| "assistant"` | 是 |  |
| `status` | `"error" \| "streaming" \| "done" \| undefined` | 否 | `streaming` while parts still arrive; `error` when the turn failed. |
| `suggestions` | `{ label: string; id?: string \| undefined; prompt?: string \| undefined; icon?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | Follow-up prompts offered under an assistant answer. |
