```typescript
interface LumiSettingsFormProps
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `about` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `accessKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `aria-activedescendant` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Identifies the currently active element when DOM focus is on a composite widget, textbox, group, or application. |
| `aria-atomic` | `Booleanish \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates whether assistive technologies will present all, or only parts of, the changed region based on the change notifications defined by the aria-relevant attribute. |
| `aria-autocomplete` | `"none" \| "both" \| "inline" \| "list" \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates whether inputting text could trigger display of one or more predictions of the user's intended value for an input and specifies how predictions would be presented if they are made. |
| `aria-braillelabel` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Defines a string value that labels the current element, which is intended to be converted into Braille. |
| `aria-brailleroledescription` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Defines a human-readable, author-localized abbreviated description for the role of an element, which is intended to be converted into Braille. |
| `aria-busy` | `Booleanish \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `aria-checked` | `boolean \| "true" \| "false" \| "mixed" \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates the current "checked" state of checkboxes, radio buttons, and other widgets. |
| `aria-colcount` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Defines the total number of columns in a table, grid, or treegrid. |
| `aria-colindex` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Defines an element's column index or position with respect to the total number of columns within a table, grid, or treegrid. |
| `aria-colindextext` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Defines a human readable text alternative of aria-colindex. |
| `aria-colspan` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Defines the number of columns spanned by a cell or gridcell within a table, grid, or treegrid. |
| `aria-controls` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Identifies the element (or elements) whose contents or presence are controlled by the current element. |
| `aria-current` | `boolean \| "date" \| "page" \| "step" \| "time" \| "true" \| "false" \| "location" \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates the element that represents the current item within a container or set of related elements. |
| `aria-describedby` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Identifies the element (or elements) that describes the object. |
| `aria-description` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Defines a string value that describes or annotates the current element. |
| `aria-details` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Identifies the element that provides a detailed, extended description for the object. |
| `aria-disabled` | `Booleanish \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates that the element is perceivable but disabled, so it is not editable or otherwise operable. |
| `aria-dropeffect` | `"none" \| "copy" \| "link" \| "execute" \| "move" \| "popup" \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates what functions can be performed when a dragged object is released on the drop target. |
| `aria-errormessage` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Identifies the element that provides an error message for the object. |
| `aria-expanded` | `Booleanish \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates whether the element, or another grouping element it controls, is currently expanded or collapsed. |
| `aria-flowto` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Identifies the next element (or elements) in an alternate reading order of content which, at the user's discretion, allows assistive technology to override the general default of reading in document source order. |
| `aria-grabbed` | `Booleanish \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates an element's "grabbed" state in a drag-and-drop operation. |
| `aria-haspopup` | `boolean \| "grid" \| "menu" \| "dialog" \| "listbox" \| "tree" \| "true" \| "false" \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates the availability and type of interactive popup element, such as menu or dialog, that can be triggered by an element. |
| `aria-hidden` | `Booleanish \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates whether the element is exposed to an accessibility API. |
| `aria-invalid` | `boolean \| "true" \| "false" \| "grammar" \| "spelling" \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates the entered value does not conform to the format expected by the application. |
| `aria-keyshortcuts` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates keyboard shortcuts that an author has implemented to activate or give focus to an element. |
| `aria-label` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Defines a string value that labels the current element. |
| `aria-labelledby` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Identifies the element (or elements) that labels the current element. |
| `aria-level` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Defines the hierarchical level of an element within a structure. |
| `aria-live` | `"off" \| "assertive" \| "polite" \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates that an element will be updated, and describes the types of updates the user agents, assistive technologies, and user can expect from the live region. |
| `aria-modal` | `Booleanish \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates whether an element is modal when displayed. |
| `aria-multiline` | `Booleanish \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates whether a text box accepts multiple lines of input or only a single line. |
| `aria-multiselectable` | `Booleanish \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates that the user may select more than one item from the current selectable descendants. |
| `aria-orientation` | `"vertical" \| "horizontal" \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates whether the element's orientation is horizontal, vertical, or unknown/ambiguous. |
| `aria-owns` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Identifies an element (or elements) in order to define a visual, functional, or contextual parent/child relationship between DOM elements where the DOM hierarchy cannot be used to represent the relationship. |
| `aria-placeholder` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Defines a short hint (a word or short phrase) intended to aid the user with data entry when the control has no value. A hint could be a sample value or a brief description of the expected format. |
| `aria-posinset` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Defines an element's number or position in the current set of listitems or treeitems. Not required if all elements in the set are present in the DOM. |
| `aria-pressed` | `boolean \| "true" \| "false" \| "mixed" \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates the current "pressed" state of toggle buttons. |
| `aria-readonly` | `Booleanish \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates that the element is not editable, but is otherwise operable. |
| `aria-relevant` | `"text" \| "all" \| "additions" \| "additions removals" \| "additions text" \| "removals" \| "removals additions" \| "removals text" \| "text additions" \| "text removals" \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates what notifications the user agent will trigger when the accessibility tree within a live region is modified. |
| `aria-required` | `Booleanish \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates that user input is required on the element before a form may be submitted. |
| `aria-roledescription` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Defines a human-readable, author-localized description for the role of an element. |
| `aria-rowcount` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Defines the total number of rows in a table, grid, or treegrid. |
| `aria-rowindex` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Defines an element's row index or position with respect to the total number of rows within a table, grid, or treegrid. |
| `aria-rowindextext` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Defines a human readable text alternative of aria-rowindex. |
| `aria-rowspan` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Defines the number of rows spanned by a cell or gridcell within a table, grid, or treegrid. |

### LumiSettingsRowProps

```typescript
interface LumiSettingsRowProps
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `about` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `accessKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `actions` | `ReactNode` | 否 | Actions and status owned by this group, rendered in its card footer. |
| `aria-activedescendant` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Identifies the currently active element when DOM focus is on a composite widget, textbox, group, or application. |
| `aria-atomic` | `Booleanish \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates whether assistive technologies will present all, or only parts of, the changed region based on the change notifications defined by the aria-relevant attribute. |
| `aria-autocomplete` | `"none" \| "both" \| "inline" \| "list" \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates whether inputting text could trigger display of one or more predictions of the user's intended value for an input and specifies how predictions would be presented if they are made. |
| `aria-braillelabel` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Defines a string value that labels the current element, which is intended to be converted into Braille. |
| `aria-brailleroledescription` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Defines a human-readable, author-localized abbreviated description for the role of an element, which is intended to be converted into Braille. |
| `aria-busy` | `Booleanish \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `aria-checked` | `boolean \| "true" \| "false" \| "mixed" \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates the current "checked" state of checkboxes, radio buttons, and other widgets. |
| `aria-colcount` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Defines the total number of columns in a table, grid, or treegrid. |
| `aria-colindex` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Defines an element's column index or position with respect to the total number of columns within a table, grid, or treegrid. |
| `aria-colindextext` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Defines a human readable text alternative of aria-colindex. |
| `aria-colspan` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Defines the number of columns spanned by a cell or gridcell within a table, grid, or treegrid. |
| `aria-controls` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Identifies the element (or elements) whose contents or presence are controlled by the current element. |
| `aria-current` | `boolean \| "date" \| "page" \| "step" \| "time" \| "true" \| "false" \| "location" \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates the element that represents the current item within a container or set of related elements. |

| `aria-grabbed` | `Booleanish \| undefined` | 否 | Indicates an element's "grabbed" state in a drag-and-drop operation. |

### TreeNode

导入路径：`@duxweb/lumi-admin/catalog`

A node of a hierarchical choice (Tree, TreeSelect, Cascader). A node with
`children: undefined` and no `isLeaf: true` may still have children that the
host loads on demand; `children: []` or `isLeaf: true` is a leaf.

```typescript

```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| `icon` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | A bundled Lumi or HugeIcons icon name drawn before the label. |
| `isLeaf` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `label` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `value` | `string \| number \| boolean` | 是 |  |

### UseAgentConversationOptions

```typescript
interface UseAgentConversationOptions
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `body` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Extra fields sent with every request, e.g. the model. |
| `commands` | `{ value: string; label: string; description?: string \| undefined; icon?: string \| undefined; prompt?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | Slash commands in the composer. One with a `prompt` fills the box; others reach `onCommand`. |
| `context` | `{ id: string; label: string; kind?: "record" \| "file" \| "page" \| "selection" \| "data" \| undefined; icon?: string \| undefined; description?: string \| undefined; data?: Record<string, unknown> \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | Context sent with every message, e.g. the page being viewed. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `endpoint` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The chat endpoint; defaults to the configured `agent.endpoint`, then `/agent/chat`. |
| `mentions` | `AdminPromptMentions \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onCommand` | `((command: AgentCommand) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | A command without a `prompt` was picked; without this handler its label is sent as a message. |
| `onSessionChange` | `((id: string \| null) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Called with the conversation to open: one picked from the list, a new one (`null`), or a started one's id. |
| `resource` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The DataProvider resource of the conversations: its list feeds `sessions`, `getOne` loads one. |
| `searchDelay` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Milliseconds the conversation search waits for typing to pause. |
| `sessionId` | `string \| null \| undefined` | 否 | The open conversation, when the host keeps it (say in the address); leave it out to keep it here. |

### AdminApp

导入路径：`@duxweb/lumi-admin/catalog`

```typescript
type AdminApp = { id: string; title?: string | undefined; titleKey?: string | undefined; subtitle?: string | undefined; subtitleKey?: string | undefined; documentTitle?: string | false | undefined; ... 8 more ...; header?: { ...; } | undefined; }
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `assistant` | `{ endpoint: string; title?: string \| undefined; placeholder?: string \| undefined; resource?: string \| undefined; suggestions?: { label: string; id?: string \| undefined; prompt?: string \| undefined; icon?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined; mentions?: { ...; } \| undefined; commands?: { ...; }[] \| undefined; default...` | 否 | The assistant panel beside every page; see `adminAssistantSchema`. |
| `documentTitle` | `string \| false \| undefined` | 否 | The browser tab's title, from `{page}` (the route's label) and `{app}` (the title above); `"{page} · {app}"` by default, `false` leaves it alone. |
| `header` | `{ actions?: { name: string; label: string; labelKey?: string \| undefined; icon?: string \| undefined; drawer?: string \| undefined; href?: string \| undefined; event?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined; } \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `id` | `string` | 是 | 记录主键。 |
| `layout` | `"single" \| "dual" \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `locales` | `{ value: string; label: string; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | Languages offered in the account menu; shown from two entries up. |
| `logo` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `search` | `"sidebar" \| "header" \| "none" \| undefined` | 否 | Where the menu search sits: atop the sidebar (default), in the header, or nowhere. |
| `subtitle` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `subtitleKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `tabs` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `title` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `titleKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `workspace` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `workspaces` | `{ value: string; label: string; description?: string \| undefined; logo?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | Workspaces (tenants, teams, stores) the user can switch between. With two or more, the sidebar head becomes a switcher; `workspace` picks the one shown first. Without them the head shows the app title and subtitle. |

### AdminAuthConfig

导入路径：`@duxweb/lumi-admin/catalog`

```typescript
type AdminAuthConfig = AdminAuthConfig
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `csrf` | `{ cookie: string; header: string; } \| undefined` | 否 | Cookie mode: the anti-forgery cookie echoed in a header on writes, e.g. `{ "cookie": "XSRF-TOKEN", "header": "X-XSRF-TOKEN" }`. |
| `data` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The backend (a `data` name) its endpoints are relative to; defaults to the panel's. |
| `endpoints` | `{ login?: string \| undefined; logout?: string \| undefined; me?: string \| undefined; refresh?: string \| undefined; register?: string \| undefined; forgotPassword?: string \| undefined; updatePassword?: string \| undefined; } \| undefined` | 否 | Session endpoints, relative to that backend. |
| `mode` | `"bearer" \| "cookie" \| undefined` | 否 | `bearer` (default) keeps the token login returns and sends it as `Authorization: Bearer …`; `cookie` relies on the server's session cookie, sent with every request, and asks `me` who is signed in. |
| `name` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `tokenKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Bearer mode: storage key of the token; defaults to one per auth name. |

### AdminBaseConfig

导入路径：`@duxweb/lumi-admin/catalog`

```typescript
type AdminBaseConfig = { data: { url: string; name?: string | undefined; live?: string | undefined; auth?: boolean | undefined; } | { name: string; url: string; live?: string | undefined; auth?: boolean | undefined; }[]; ... 11 more ...; cacheKey?: string | undefined; }
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `access` | `{ name?: string \| undefined; separator?: ":" \| "." \| undefined; format?: string \| undefined; } \| { name: string; separator?: ":" \| "." \| undefined; format?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | The permission rules; `resource:action` when left out. |
| `auth` | `{ name?: string \| undefined; mode?: "bearer" \| "cookie" \| undefined; tokenKey?: string \| undefined; csrf?: { cookie: string; header: string; } \| undefined; data?: string \| undefined; endpoints?: { ...; } \| undefined; } \| { ...; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | The sign-ins; one with the default endpoints when left out. |
| `basename` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Where the site is served when not at the root, e.g. `/console`; every panel path is below it. |
| `cacheKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Storage namespace for the manifest cache and workspace; each panel keeps its own below it. |
| `data` | `{ url: string; name?: string \| undefined; live?: string \| undefined; auth?: boolean \| undefined; } \| { name: string; url: string; live?: string \| undefined; auth?: boolean \| undefined; }[]` | 是 | The backends. |
| `defaultPanel` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The panel `/` opens; defaults to the first. |
| `el` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | CSS selector of the mount node; defaults to `#app`. |
| `frameOrigins` | `string[] \| undefined` | 否 | Sites an `Iframe` element or a `page.iframe` route may show, e.g. `["https://report.example.com"]`; this site always may. Without it any https site may. |
| `i18n` | `{ locale: string; fallbackLocale?: string \| undefined; translations?: Record<string, string \| Record<string, string>> \| undefined; format?: { currency: string; currencyUnit: "major" \| "minor"; timeZone: string; dateTimeInput: "iso-utc"; } \| undefined; } \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `modules` | `string[] \| undefined` | 否 | Extension modules the base imports before it mounts: plain ES modules whose default export receives the runtime (React, Lumi, UI, Admin, zod) and returns the components, actions and callbacks they add. |
| `packKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Key of packed manifests, pages and themes. The backend may answer `{ "packed": "…" }` instead of readable JSON so the text does not show in the network panel; that works without any setting here, and this is the key when the backend masks the packed bytes with one. It hides documents from a casual look only: this file is itself readable. |
| `panels` | `{ manifest: string \| { app: { id: string; title?: string \| undefined; titleKey?: string \| undefined; subtitle?: string \| undefined; subtitleKey?: string \| undefined; documentTitle?: string \| false \| undefined; ... 8 more ...; header?: { ...; } \| undefined; }; routes: AdminRoute[]; }; ... 11 more ...; shellStorageKey...` | 是 | The back offices this deployment serves, chosen by path. |
| `theme` | `string \| { mode?: "light" \| "dark" \| "system" \| undefined; primary?: string \| undefined; brand?: string \| undefined; radius?: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined; fontSize?: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined; ... 5 more ...; info?: string \| undefined; } \| undefined` | 否 | Theme every panel uses unless it names its own. |

### AdminExtension

What an extension module contributes. A module written as plain JavaScript
— no bundler — default-exports `(runtime, { config, http }) => AdminExtension`,
so it reaches React, the Lumi hooks and the UI components through `runtime`
and shares the base's single React instance; `http` is the app's signed
request client for the data providers it adds.

```typescript
type AdminExtension = AdminExtension
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProviders` | `Record<string, AccessProvider> \| undefined` | 否 | Access providers by `access` name, replacing the permission-string rule. |
| `actions` | `Record<string, AdminExtensionAction> \| undefined` | 否 | Definitions of the actions in `handlers`, so pages that use them are validated like built-in ones. A handler without one is still accepted in JSON, with its params unchecked. |
| `agent` | `AgentConfig \| undefined` | 否 | Agent chat requests: the default endpoint, the data source and extra headers. |
| `assistant` | `ReactNode` | 否 | A panel beside the page, such as an AI assistant. The shell renders its frame and a header toggle; the content is entirely the host's. |
| `assistantCommands` | `{ value: string; label: string; description?: string \| undefined; icon?: string \| undefined; prompt?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | / commands added to the manifest's `app.assistant`. |
| `assistantDefaultOpen` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Whether the assistant panel starts open for a reader who has not toggled it yet; defaults to open. The reader's own choice is remembered. |
| `assistantMentions` | `AdminPromptMentions \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Records each accepted write and lists the record history; optional. |
| `authProviders` | `Record<string, AuthProvider> \| undefined` | 否 | Auth providers by `auth` name, replacing the simple convention for the panels that use it. |
| `components` | `Record<string, AdminExtensionComponent> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `dataProviders` | `Record<string, DataProvider \| AdminRequestHooks> \| undefined` | 否 | Data providers by `data` name, reached with `dataProviderName`; `default` stands for the mounted panel's own backend. Build them on the `http` the extension context hands over, so they carry the reader's credentials, locale and tenant. An entry may instead give only `{ onRequest, onResponse }`: the provider the config already builds for that name keeps its address and gains the hooks. On the panel's backend they reach every request to it, sign-in, uploads, the agent, the event stream and served pages included. |
| `drawers` | `AdminRoutedDrawerRegistry \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `env` | `Record<string, string \| number \| boolean \| null> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `handlers` | `Record<string, ActionHandler> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `headerEnd` | `ReactNode` | 否 | Rendered on the header's trailing edge — the design's notifications bell and anything else app-wide the shell cannot supply the feature for. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | 翻译、切换语言和格式化。 |
| `liveProvider` | `LiveProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Pushes backend changes; with it, queries refetch what an event touches. |
| `loadPageModule` | `AdminPageModuleLoader \| undefined` | 否 | Loads a `page.module` route's component; defaults to `import()` and the module's default export. Each URL loads once. |
| `manifestLoader` | `LumiJsonLoader<{ app: { id: string; title?: string \| undefined; titleKey?: string \| undefined; subtitle?: string \| undefined; subtitleKey?: string \| undefined; documentTitle?: string \| false \| undefined; ... 8 more ...; header?: { ...; } \| undefined; }; routes: AdminRoute[]; }> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `menuBadges` | `Readonly<Record<string, ShellMenuBadge>> \| undefined` | 否 | Live counts beside menu items, keyed by route name. |
| `notificationProvider` | `NotificationProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws `notify` notices; without one the action is a no-op. |
| `onEvent` | `AdminEventHandler \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onWorkspaceChange` | `((value: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Called when the user picks another workspace in the sidebar switcher (`app.workspaces`). |
| `packKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Key of packed manifests, pages and themes, when the backend masks them with one. A backend may answer `{ "packed": "…" }` instead of readable JSON (see `packDocument` in `@duxweb/lumi/catalog`); it is unpacked either way, and this is only needed when a key was used. |
| `routes` | `RouteObject[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `shell` | `ComponentType<{ children?: ReactNode \| undefined; }> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `sidebarFooter` | `ReactNode` | 否 | Rendered at the foot of the navigation sidebar, below the menu. |
| `specLoader` | `LumiJsonLoader<Spec> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The tenant (workspace) requests belong to; queries refetch when it changes. |
| `themeLoader` | `LumiJsonLoader<{ mode?: "light" \| "dark" \| "system" \| undefined; primary?: string \| undefined; brand?: string \| undefined; radius?: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined; fontSize?: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined; ... 5 more ...; info?: string \| undefined; }> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws `toast` feedback and automatic error reports; without one they are no-ops. |
| `upload` | `UploadOptions \| undefined` | 否 | 全局上传地址、请求头和允许的目标来源。 |
| `validator` | `Validator \| undefined` | 否 | 统一的数据校验器，可接 AJV、Zod、Valibot 或自定义实现。 |

| `remember` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Offer "Remember me" (default true); the choice reaches the auth provider's `login` as `remember`. |
| `subtitle` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Line under the "Welcome back" heading. |

### AdminRequestHooks

Request hooks for a backend the config already names; see `HttpClientOptions`.

```typescript
type AdminRequestHooks = AdminRequestHooks
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `onRequest` | `((request: HttpOutgoingRequest) => HttpOutgoingRequest \| void \| Promise<HttpOutgoingRequest \| void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Last look at a request before it leaves, after the credentials, locale, tenant and JSON encoding: add or rewrite headers, sign the method, URL and body. Runs again for the resend after a 401 refresh. Change the request in place or return a new one. |
| `onResponse` | `((response: Response, request: HttpOutgoingRequest) => Response \| void \| Promise<Response \| void>) \| undefined` | 否 | First look at the final answer, whatever its status: check its signature, decrypt it, or reshape it. Return another Response to replace it; throw to fail the request. |

### MountAdminExtensions

Everything JSON cannot carry, supplied by a compiled host or by a page script
(`window.LumiAdminSetup`): callbacks, components and providers. A provider
set here replaces the one built from the config: an auth provider signs in
against a backend that does not speak the simple convention, and every
request (data, uploads, agent, live) carries its credentials. `upload` and
`agent` options merge over the defaults.

```typescript
type MountAdminExtensions = MountAdminExtensions
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProviders` | `Record<string, AccessProvider> \| undefined` | 否 | Access providers by `access` name, replacing the permission-string rule. |
| `agent` | `AgentConfig \| undefined` | 否 | Agent chat requests: the default endpoint, the data source and extra headers. |
| `assistant` | `ReactNode` | 否 | A panel beside the page, such as an AI assistant. The shell renders its frame and a header toggle; the content is entirely the host's. |
| `assistantCommands` | `{ value: string; label: string; description?: string \| undefined; icon?: string \| undefined; prompt?: string \| undefined; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | / commands added to the manifest's `app.assistant`. |
| `assistantDefaultOpen` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Whether the assistant panel starts open for a reader who has not toggled it yet; defaults to open. The reader's own choice is remembered. |
| `assistantMentions` | `AdminPromptMentions \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Records each accepted write and lists the record history; optional. |
| `authProviders` | `Record<string, AuthProvider> \| undefined` | 否 | Auth providers by `auth` name, replacing the simple convention for the panels that use it. |
| `catalog` | `Catalog<SchemaDefinition<SchemaType<string, unknown>, SchemaType<string, unknown>>, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `dataProviders` | `Record<string, DataProvider \| AdminRequestHooks> \| undefined` | 否 | Data providers by `data` name, reached with `dataProviderName`; `default` stands for the mounted panel's own backend. Build them on the `http` the extension context hands over, so they carry the reader's credentials, locale and tenant. An entry may instead give only `{ onRequest, onResponse }`: the provider the config already builds for that name keeps its address and gains the hooks. On the panel's backend they reach every request to it, sign-in, uploads, the agent, the event stream and served pages included. |
| `drawers` | `AdminRoutedDrawerRegistry \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `env` | `Record<string, string \| number \| boolean \| null> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `handlers` | `Record<string, ActionHandler> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `headerEnd` | `ReactNode` | 否 | Rendered on the header's trailing edge — the design's notifications bell and anything else app-wide the shell cannot supply the feature for. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | 翻译、切换语言和格式化。 |
| `liveProvider` | `LiveProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Pushes backend changes; with it, queries refetch what an event touches. |
| `loadPageModule` | `AdminPageModuleLoader \| undefined` | 否 | Loads a `page.module` route's component; defaults to `import()` and the module's default export. Each URL loads once. |
| `manifestLoader` | `LumiJsonLoader<{ app: { id: string; title?: string \| undefined; titleKey?: string \| undefined; subtitle?: string \| undefined; subtitleKey?: string \| undefined; documentTitle?: string \| false \| undefined; ... 8 more ...; header?: { ...; } \| undefined; }; routes: AdminRoute[]; }> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `menuBadges` | `Readonly<Record<string, ShellMenuBadge>> \| undefined` | 否 | Live counts beside menu items, keyed by route name. |
| `notificationProvider` | `NotificationProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws `notify` notices; without one the action is a no-op. |
| `onEvent` | `AdminEventHandler \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onWorkspaceChange` | `((value: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Called when the user picks another workspace in the sidebar switcher (`app.workspaces`). |
| `packKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Key of packed manifests, pages and themes, when the backend masks them with one. A backend may answer `{ "packed": "…" }` instead of readable JSON (see `packDocument` in `@duxweb/lumi/catalog`); it is unpacked either way, and this is only needed when a key was used. |
| `registry` | `ComponentRegistry \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `routes` | `RouteObject[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `shell` | `ComponentType<{ children?: ReactNode \| undefined; }> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `sidebarFooter` | `ReactNode` | 否 | Rendered at the foot of the navigation sidebar, below the menu. |
| `specLoader` | `LumiJsonLoader<Spec> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The tenant (workspace) requests belong to; queries refetch when it changes. |
| `themeLoader` | `LumiJsonLoader<{ mode?: "light" \| "dark" \| "system" \| undefined; primary?: string \| undefined; brand?: string \| undefined; radius?: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined; fontSize?: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| undefined; ... 5 more ...; info?: string \| undefined; }> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws `toast` feedback and automatic error reports; without one they are no-ops. |
| `upload` | `UploadOptions \| undefined` | 否 | 全局上传地址、请求头和允许的目标来源。 |
| `validator` | `Validator \| undefined` | 否 | 统一的数据校验器，可接 AJV、Zod、Valibot 或自定义实现。 |
